### Performance Improvements

* **genshin-engine:** an instanced mesh keeps its matrices in a storage buffer, uploaded only when they change ([88006c5](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/88006c5cc36717c0300946f00299c906275b6fea))
* **genshin-engine:** the world compiles its first view before ready and no more, so the whole-scene warm draw is deleted ([b66a41c](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/b66a41c8d4e928881dbad9cafe42b589ccdd6c98))
* **genshin-parity:** the colour pass's label similarity is reduced on the parity page's WebGPU device, so the Surface pass takes a sixth less wall time and about half its Node CPU, with every printed figure unchanged ([94590f7](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/94590f7573d2d3b4c35d09a41971f3516822395d))
* **genshin-parity:** the surface pass's structure statistics are reduced on the parity page's WebGPU device, its blurs as compute passes and only partial sums read back, so windrise's surface pass takes two to three minutes of wall time and about a sixth of its Node CPU, with every figure matching the CPU reference ([f168bd7](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/f168bd79be14c3db2700afb8d46b1c3d73dfe2f8))
* **genshin-world:** a kit's module loads only as its character joins the party ([f0e780f](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/f0e780f9b7d1a20a11c17396634941a840283a82))

* **genshin-world:** a resident's ground height is read once per spot, not on every frame it is tested ([ed362e8](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/ed362e88110705a9bcae24bae29ee35f28ea293d))
* **genshin-world:** a terrain tile compiles before it shows, so the walk's worst frame drops from about 67 to 35 ms ([7510db1](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/7510db1cc08758f2418ef10e645eca3993093c14))
* **genshin-world:** a witness family's still parts are drawn as instances, one per submesh and material ([2fd75c9](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/2fd75c9679a9f9276e2329407a8e377742953cfe))
* **genshin-world:** an arriving ground rebuilds only the tiles it reaches, 33 on the crossing into Liyue where it was every one held ([036e9d0](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/036e9d00d112a5947d9f7519ceff1e5fbd3edc90))
